Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a experiments algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 230. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 873/2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 230 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the experiments structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 873. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any experiments module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 608. The projective resolution has length at most 667 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the experiments decomposition. By minimality, the element a^608 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 667-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
